, then the rotation drift satisfies

\|\bm{R}^{*}-\bm{I}_{r}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{A})+\sigma_{r}(\bm{B})}\|\bm{A}-\bm{B}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

C.2
Proof of Theorem
1

We prove in this subsection the stronger contraction statement under the localization
\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. Accordingly, throughout the proof we invoke Assumptions
1
,
2
,
B.5
, and
B.6
with
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. The
\ell_{2}
-only part of Theorem
1
, where only Assumptions
1
–
2
are imposed with
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(2)}(\epsilon)
, is proved later in Section
C.4
by the same argument after removing the row-wise estimates.
Throughout this proof, we use the specialization
\lambda=\alpha
from Lemma
2
and the constant step size
\eta^{t}\equiv\eta=\{10(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min}\}^{-1}
from Theorem
1
.
For each
t\geq 0
, let

\bm{R}_{t}^{*}=\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r}}\|\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},\quad\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}:=\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}\quad\text{, and }\quad\bm{E}_{t}=\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}-\bm{Z}^{*}.

By the orthogonal Procrustes optimality condition (Lemma
4
), we know
(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t{\intercal}}\bm{Z}^{*}
. Then the by construction,
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t})={\bm{0}}
and
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
.

For the
\ell_{2}
part of the theorem, the initialization bound (
8
) gives the base case at
t=0
. For the
\ell_{\infty}
part, the bounds (
8
) and (
10
) give the base case at
t=0
. Next, we show that provided (
9
) and
11
hold for
0,1,2,\dots,t
, (
9
) and
11
also hold for the iterate in the
(t+1)
th step.

For
s\in[0,1]
, define
\bm{Z}_{t}(s):=\bm{Z}^{*}+s\bm{E}_{t}
.
Because
\bm{Z}_{t}(s)
is already aligned with
\bm{Z}^{*}
,

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq s\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}},\qquad dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq s\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}.

With the induction hypothesis, we know

n^{-1/2}dist_{2}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq n^{-1/2}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi_{n}\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sqrt{n}},

(C.1)

and

\begin{aligned} dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\end{aligned}.

(C.2)

Hence, by the theorem assumptions
\phi_{n}\leq\epsilon/2
and, for the
\ell_{\infty}
part,
\psi_{n}\leq\epsilon/2
, the bounds (
8
), (
10
), (
C.1
), and (
C.2
) imply that
\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(2)}(\epsilon)
for all
s\in[0,1]
in Step 1, and
\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
for all
s\in[0,1]
in Step 2.

Let
\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}=\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}-\bm{Z}^{*}
.
Recall that in (
5
), we have established
\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}=\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}-\frac{\eta^{t}}{n}\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\lambda}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}_{t}^{*})
. Since
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
,
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t})={\bm{0}}
by the alignment property, and
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
because
\bm{M}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})=0
, we have

\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}=\bm{E}_{t}-\frac{\eta}{n}\big\{\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t})-\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\big\}.

We now split the proof into the
\ell_{2}
- and
\ell_{\infty}
-parts.

Step 1:
\ell_{2}
-error contraction.

We prove (
9
) for the
(t+1)
th iterate. Since
dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
,
it suffices to bound
\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
.

For
s\in[0,1]
, define

\mathcal{A}_{t}(s):=n^{-1}\Big\{\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))-2\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))\otimes\bm{I}_{r}\Big\},\qquad\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}:=\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{A}_{t}(s)\,ds,

and

\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}:=\frac{2}{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))\,ds.

By the fundamental theorem of calculus (Theorem 4.2 in
28
, Chapter XIII),

\mathrm{vec}\!\Big(\big\{\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t})-\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\big\}^{\intercal}\Big)=\Big(\int_{0}^{1}\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))\,ds\Big)\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}).

Using
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})=2\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})\bm{Z}
, we therefore obtain

\mathrm{vec}\!\big((\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)})^{\intercal}\big)=(\bm{I}_{nr}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal})-\eta(\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\otimes\bm{I}_{r})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}).

Since
(\bm{B}\otimes\bm{A}^{\intercal})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{X})=\mathrm{vec}(\bm{A}\bm{X}\bm{B})
, the second term equals
\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t})
. Hence

\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\big\|(\bm{I}_{nr}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal})\big\|+\eta\|\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=:\gamma_{1,t}+\gamma_{2,t}.

We first bound
\gamma_{1,t}
. Since
\sigma_{r}(n^{-1/2}\bm{Z}^{*})^{2}={\sigma_{r}(\bm{X}^{*})}/{n}=\sigma_{\min}
, apply Lemma
2
to get, for every
s\in[0,1]
,

\lambda_{\min}\big(\mathcal{A}_{t}(s)\big)\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-4n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-4n^{-1}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Since
\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}=\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{A}_{t}(s)\,ds
, we can further obtain

\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\geq\int_{0}^{1}\lambda_{\min}\big(\mathcal{A}_{t}(s)\big)\,ds\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-4n^{-1}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

By the induction hypothesis
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
and
\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq r\sigma_{1}(\bm{X}^{*})=nr\kappa\sigma_{\min}
,

n^{-1}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\phi_{n}^{2}\,n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\phi_{n}^{2}r\kappa\sigma_{\min}\leq c_{0}^{2}\frac{\alpha^{2}}{\kappa}\sigma_{\min}\leq c_{0}^{2}\alpha\sigma_{\min},

where we used
\phi_{n}\leq c_{0}\alpha/(\kappa\sqrt{r})
,
0<\alpha<1
, and
\kappa\geq 1
.
Hence, after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary,

\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\geq\frac{7}{8}\alpha\sigma_{\min}.

(C.3)

Next, we bound
\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})
. This upper is only needed to ensure that
\bm{I}_{nr}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}
remains a contraction. For any
\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
with
\|\bm{W}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1
, Assumption
1
gives

\displaystyle n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{Z}}^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\bm{Z}_{t}(s)^{\intercal})[\bm{W},\bm{W}]-2n^{-1}\langle\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s)),\bm{W}\bm{W}^{\intercal}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\beta n^{-1}\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{Z}_{t}(s)}(\bm{W})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq 4\beta n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\|^{2},

where the last step uses
\|\mathcal{P}_{\bm{Z}}(\bm{W})\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 2\|\bm{Z}\|\,\|\bm{W}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
.
Also, we note that

\frac{1}{n}\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|^{2}+\frac{2}{n}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq 2\kappa\sigma_{\min}+2c_{0}^{2}\kappa\sigma_{\min}\leq\frac{17}{8}\kappa\sigma_{\min},

after shrinking
c_{0}
so that
2c_{0}^{2}\leq 1/8
, where we used

\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|^{2}=\sigma_{1}(\bm{X}^{*})=n\kappa\sigma_{\min}
.

For the penalty term, recall

p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})=\frac{\alpha n^{2}}{4}\|\bm{M}^{*}(\bm{Z})\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},\qquad\bm{M}^{*}(\bm{Z})=n^{-1}\big\{(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\bm{Z}-\bm{Z}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\big\}.

Since
\bm{M}^{*}(\cdot)
is linear, it follows that

D\bm{M}^{*}(\bm{Z})[\bm{W}]=n^{-1}\big\{(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\bm{W}-\bm{W}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\big\},\qquad D^{2}\bm{M}^{*}(\bm{Z})=0.

This hence gives

\displaystyle n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{Z}}^{2}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))[\bm{W},\bm{W}]

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha n}{2}\|D\bm{M}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))[\bm{W}]\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha}{2n}\|(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\bm{W}-\bm{W}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|^{2}\|\bm{W}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}=2\alpha\kappa\sigma_{\min}.

Combining the bounds for the loss part and the penalty part yields

\lambda_{\max}\big(\mathcal{A}_{t}(s)\big)\leq 4\beta\cdot\frac{17}{8}\kappa\sigma_{\min}+2\alpha\kappa\sigma_{\min}\leq 9(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min},

for all
s\in[0,1]
. Hence the same bound holds for
\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}
that
\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\leq 9(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min}
.
Since
\eta\leq\{10(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min}\}^{-1}
, we have
0\leq\eta\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\leq\tfrac{9}{10}
.
Thus all eigenvalues of
\bm{I}_{nr}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}
lie in
[0,1)
, and therefore

\big\|(\bm{I}_{nr}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal})\big\|\leq\bigl(1-\eta\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t})\bigr)\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
.
Using (
C.3
), we conclude

\gamma_{1,t}\leq\left(1-\frac{7}{8}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\right)\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(C.4)

We next bound
\gamma_{2,t}
. By the induction hypothesis,

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t},\bm{Z}^{*})=\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi_{n}\sqrt{r}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\leq c_{0}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|,

where the last step uses
\phi_{n}\leq c_{0}\alpha/(\kappa\sqrt{r})
and
\alpha/\kappa\leq 1
.
Since
dist_{2}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
, Lemma
5
implies

\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\bm{Z}_{t}(s)^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(2+c_{0})\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\,dist_{2}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq C\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\,\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

uniformly over
s\in[0,1]
. Therefore, using
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
and Assumption
2
within
\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
,

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{n}\int_{0}^{1}\|\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))-\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|\,ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2L_{2}}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\bm{Z}_{t}(s)^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq CL_{2}\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{n}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where we use
\|\cdot\|\leq\|\cdot\|_{\mathrm{F}}
.
Consequently, we have
\gamma_{2,t}\leq\eta\|\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\|\,\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq CL_{2}\eta\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{n}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
.
We now check that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{n}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\phi_{n}\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\phi_{n}\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\leq c_{0}\alpha\sigma_{\min},

(C.5)

where we used
{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}/{\sqrt{n}}=\sqrt{\kappa\sigma_{\min}}
and
{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}/{\sqrt{n}}\leq\sqrt{r\kappa\sigma_{\min}}
. Hence, this establishes that

\gamma_{2,t}\leq CL_{2}c_{0}\,\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

After shrinking
c_{0}
further so that
CL_{2}c_{0}\leq 1/2
, we obtain

\gamma_{2,t}\leq\frac{\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{2}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(C.6)

Combining (
C.4
) and (
C.6
), we arrive at

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{8}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\right)\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\rho\,\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where
\rho=1-\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}/4
. Since
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t},\bm{Z}^{*})
, iterating the recursion and using
dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{0},\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\phi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
yields

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\rho^{t+1}\phi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

This proves
\ell_{2}
error contraction in (
9
) for the
(t+1)
th iterate under local region
\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
.

Step 2:
\ell_{\infty}
-error contraction.

We first control the alignment error
\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}=\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}-\bm{Z}^{*}
, and then transfer the bound to the next optimal alignment
\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}
. As in Step 1, the whole interpolation segment
\{\bm{Z}_{t}(s):0\leq s\leq 1\}
lies in
\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
.

We decompose the rowwise error into the term arising from alignment and the drift of the optimal alignment as follows

\displaystyle dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\bm{Z}^{*})

\displaystyle=\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}

(C.7)

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}-\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\big\|\bm{I}_{r}-(\bm{R}_{t}^{*})^{-1}\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}\big\|.

We first bound
\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}
.
Recall that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}=\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}
, so by Lemma
4
,
(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t{\intercal}}\bm{Z}^{*}
. Equivalently, we know

(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\bm{E}_{t}=\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}
.
Therefore, for every
s\in[0,1]
,

\bm{M}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))=n^{-1}\big\{(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\bm{Z}_{t}(s)-\bm{Z}_{t}(s)^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\big\}=sn^{-1}\big\{(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\bm{E}_{t}-\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\big\}=0.

It follows that

\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))=0,\qquad\frac{d}{ds}\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))=\nabla_{\bm{Z}}^{2}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))[\bm{E}_{t}]=0,

for all
s\in[0,1]
. Thus, the penalty contributes nothing to the directional Hessian action along
\bm{E}_{t}
.

For
i,j\in[n]
, we define

\mathcal{H}_{ij}(\bm{Z}):=n^{-1}\big\{\nabla_{\bm{z}_{i}\bm{z}_{j}}^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})-2G_{ij}(\bm{Z})\bm{I}_{r}\big\},\qquad\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ij,t}:=\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{H}_{ij}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))\,ds.

Using the same fundamental theorem of calculus expansion as in Step 1, together with

\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}=\frac{2}{n}\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))\,ds
, we obtain for each
i\in[n]
,

(\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)})_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}=\big(\bm{I}_{r}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ii,t}\big)(\bm{E}_{t})_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}-\eta\sum_{j\neq i}\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ij,t}(\bm{E}_{t})_{j,\cdot}^{\intercal}-\eta(\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\bm{E}_{t})_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}.

(C.8)

Consequently, we have the following decomposition

\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\delta_{1,t}+\delta_{2,t}+\delta_{3,t},

where
\delta_{1,t}
,
\delta_{2,t}
, and
\delta_{3,t}
denote the maxima over
i\in[n]
of the three terms on the right-hand side of (
C.8
).

We first bound
\delta_{1,t}
.
By Lemma
3
, for every
s\in[0,1]
and every
i\in[n]
,

\lambda_{\min}\big(\mathcal{H}_{ii}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))\big)\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-2n^{-1}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

and

\lambda_{\max}\big(\mathcal{H}_{ii}(\bm{Z}_{t}(s))\big)\leq\beta\kappa\sigma_{\min}+2n^{-1}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

As in Step 1, we have by the induction hypothesis that

n^{-1}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\phi_{n}^{2}r\kappa\sigma_{\min}\leq c_{0}^{2}\alpha\sigma_{\min},

where we used scaling condition
\phi_{n}\leq c_{0}\alpha/(\kappa\sqrt{r})
. Hence, after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, we get

\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ii,t})\geq\frac{7}{8}\alpha\sigma_{\min},\qquad\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ii,t})\leq 1.01(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min},

uniformly over
i\in[n]
. Since
\eta\leq\{10(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min}\}^{-1}
, we have

0\leq\eta\lambda_{\max}(\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ii,t})\leq\frac{1}{5}.

Therefore all eigenvalues of
\bm{I}_{r}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ii,t}
lie in
[0,1)
, and subsequently

\|\bm{I}_{r}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ii,t}\|=1-\eta\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{ii,t})\leq 1-\frac{7}{8}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}.

Taking the maximum over
i
yields

\delta_{1,t}\leq\Big(1-\frac{7}{8}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\Big)\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}.

(C.9)

Next, we bound
\delta_{2,t}
. Fix
i\in[n]
, vectors
\{\bm{u}_{j}\}_{j\neq i}\subset\mathbb{R}^{r}
, and
\bm{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
with
\|\bm{a}\|=1
. Define
\bm{W}_{1}:=\bm{e}_{i}\bm{a}^{\intercal}
,
(\bm{W}_{2})_{j,\cdot}:=\bm{u}_{j}^{\intercal}\ \ (j\neq i)
, and
(\bm{W}_{2})_{i,\cdot}:=0
.
Then we note that

\nabla_{\bm{Z}}^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})[\bm{W}_{1},\bm{W}_{2}]=\nabla_{\bm{X}}^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})\big[\mathcal{P}_{\bm{Z}}(\bm{W}_{1}),\mathcal{P}_{\bm{Z}}(\bm{W}_{2})\big]+2\langle\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}),\bm{W}_{1}\bm{W}_{2}^{\intercal}\rangle.

Since
\bm{W}_{1}
is supported on row
i
and
(\bm{W}_{2})_{i,\cdot}=0
, we have
\langle\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}),\bm{W}_{1}\bm{W}_{2}^{\intercal}\rangle=\sum_{j\neq i}G_{ij}(\bm{Z})\,\bm{a}^{\intercal}\bm{u}_{j}
, and therefore

\sum_{j\neq i}\bm{a}^{\intercal}\mathcal{H}_{ij}(\bm{Z})\bm{u}_{j}=n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{X}}^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})\big[\mathcal{P}_{\bm{Z}}(\bm{W}_{1}),\mathcal{P}_{\bm{Z}}(\bm{W}_{2})\big].

Assumption
B.5
now gives

\Big|\sum_{j\neq i}\bm{a}^{\intercal}\mathcal{H}_{ij}(\bm{Z})\bm{u}_{j}\Big|\leq\beta n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}_{i,\cdot}\|\,\|\bm{Z}\|\,\|\bm{W}_{2}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\beta n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}_{i,\cdot}\|\,\|\bm{Z}\|\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}\|\bm{u}_{j}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}.

Taking the supremum over
\|\bm{a}\|=1
, we arrive at

\Big\|\sum_{j\neq i}\mathcal{H}_{ij}(\bm{Z})\bm{u}_{j}\Big\|\leq\beta n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}_{i,\cdot}\|\,\|\bm{Z}\|\Big(\sum_{j\neq i}\|\bm{u}_{j}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}.

Applying this with
\bm{u}_{j}=(\bm{E}_{t})_{j,\cdot}^{\intercal}
, integrating over
s\in[0,1]
, and taking the maximum over
i
, we obtain

\delta_{2,t}\leq\eta\beta n^{-1}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\|\,\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Now, by the induction on
\ell_{2}
error contraction
{\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}/{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}\leq\phi_{n}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}/{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}\leq\phi_{n}\sqrt{r}\leq c_{0}{\alpha}/{\kappa}\leq c_{0}
, it then holds that
\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\|\leq\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|+\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(1+c_{0})\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|
, and, by the induction hypothesis
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\epsilon/2\;\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}
,

\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\big(1+\frac{\epsilon}{2}\big)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Using
\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|^{2}=\sigma_{1}(\bm{X}^{*})=n\kappa\sigma_{\min}
, we conclude that

\delta_{2,t}\leq C\eta\beta\sqrt{\frac{\kappa\sigma_{\min}}{n}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(C.10)

Finally, we bound
\delta_{3,t}
. Using
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
, Assumption
B.6
, and
\|\bm{A}\bm{B}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\|\bm{A}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{B}\|
, we have

\displaystyle\delta_{3,t}

\displaystyle\leq\eta\|\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\eta\|\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta L_{\infty}}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\bm{Z}_{t}(s)^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{2\to\infty}\,\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Expanding the matrix difference by telescoping as

\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\bm{Z}_{t}(s)^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}=s(\bm{Z}^{*}\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}+\bm{E}_{t}\bm{Z}^{*{\intercal}})+s^{2}\bm{E}_{t}\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal},

we obtain

\displaystyle\|\bm{Z}_{t}(s)\bm{Z}_{t}(s)^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{2\to\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{Z}^{*}\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{E}_{t}\bm{Z}^{*{\intercal}}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{E}_{t}\bm{E}_{t}^{\intercal}\|_{2\to\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|+\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+(1+c_{0})\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty},

where in the last step we used
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq c_{0}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|
. Plugging this into the previous display gives

\delta_{3,t}\leq\frac{2\eta L_{\infty}}{n}\Big\{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+C\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\Big\}.

We next bound the two terms separately.
Since
{\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}/{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}\leq\phi_{n}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}/{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}\leq\phi_{n}\sqrt{r}\leq c_{0}{\alpha}/{\kappa}
, one can obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}=\sqrt{\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n\kappa}}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\times\frac{\kappa\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}\leq c_{0}\alpha\sqrt{\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n\kappa}}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where we used
\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|=\sqrt{n\kappa\sigma_{\min}}
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\,\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\times\frac{\kappa\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\alpha\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}\leq c_{0}\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Hence, one has

\delta_{3,t}\leq CL_{\infty}c_{0}\,\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}+C\eta\alpha\sqrt{\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n\kappa}}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

After shrinking
c_{0}
further so that
CL_{\infty}c_{0}\leq 1/16
, we arrive at

\delta_{3,t}\leq\frac{1}{16}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}+C\eta\alpha\sqrt{\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{n\kappa}}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(C.11)

Substituting the bounds for
\delta_{1,t}
,
\delta_{2,t}
, and
\delta_{3,t}
obtain in (
C.9
), (
C.10
), and (
C.11
) into (
C.8
), and using
\alpha\leq\beta
and
\kappa\geq 1
, we get

\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\Big(1-\frac{13}{16}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\Big)\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}+C\eta\beta\sqrt{\frac{\kappa\sigma_{\min}}{n}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

By the error contraction established in Step 1, we know
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\rho^{t}\phi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\rho^{t}\phi_{n}\sqrt{nr\kappa\sigma_{\min}}
.
Therefore,

\beta\sqrt{\frac{\kappa\sigma_{\min}}{n}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\beta\kappa\sqrt{r}\,\sigma_{\min}\,\rho^{t}\phi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Using the theorem scaling condition
\tfrac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{3/2}\sqrt{r}\,\tfrac{\phi_{n}}{\psi_{n}}\leq c_{0}
,
and shrinking
c_{0}
further so that
Cc_{0}\leq 1/16
, we obtain
C\beta\kappa\sqrt{r}\,\sigma_{\min}\,\phi_{n}\leq\alpha\sigma_{\min}\psi_{n}/16
.
We therefore get

\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\Big(1-\frac{13}{16}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\Big)\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}+\frac{1}{16}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Invoking the induction hypothesis
\|\bm{E}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}
, we arrive at

\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\Big(1-\frac{3}{4}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\Big)\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq(3\rho-2)\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

(C.12)

where the last inequality follows from
\rho=1-\tfrac{1}{4}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}
.

It remains to transfer this bound from the alignment
\bm{R}_{t}^{*}
to the next optimal alignment
\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}
. By orthogonal invariance,
(\bm{R}_{t}^{*})^{-1}\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}
is the solution to

\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r}}\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}\bm{R}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Now that

\frac{\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\leq\rho^{t+1}\phi_{n}\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sqrt{n\sigma_{\min}}}\leq\rho^{t+1}\phi_{n}\sqrt{r\kappa},

the theorem scaling condition
\tfrac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{3/2}\sqrt{r}\,\tfrac{\phi_{n}}{\psi_{n}}\leq c_{0}
and
\kappa\geq 1
imply
\sqrt{r\,\kappa}\,\tfrac{\phi_{n}}{\psi_{n}}\leq c_{0}
, which further yields

\frac{\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\leq c_{0}\,\rho^{t+1}\psi_{n}.

In particular, after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, we have

\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 0.01\,\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})
, so Lemma
6
applies and yields

\displaystyle\big\|(\bm{R}_{t}^{*})^{-1}\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}-\bm{I}_{r}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*})+\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1.01}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where the last step uses Weyl’s inequality and

\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 0.01\,\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})
.

Returning to (
C.7
), we obtain

\displaystyle dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\bm{Z}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\big\|(\bm{R}_{t}^{*})^{-1}\bm{R}_{t+1}^{*}-\bm{I}_{r}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}+C\frac{\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\Big(\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}\Big).

Using the bounds already proved, we get
{\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}/{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\leq\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}\rho^{t+1}\leq\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}
.
Therefore, one can obtain

\displaystyle dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\bm{Z}^{*})\leq

\displaystyle\big(1+C\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}\big)\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}+C\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}\rho^{t+1}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}

(C.13)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big(1+C\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}\big)(3\rho-2)\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+C\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}\rho^{t+1}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{t+1}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

where the second inequality is due to
\|\bm{E}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq(3-2\rho)\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}
from (
C.12
). For the third inequality, we note that from
\tfrac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{3/2}\sqrt{r}\tfrac{\phi_{n}}{\psi_{n}}\leq c_{0}
,
\eta=\{10(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min}\}^{-1}
, and
\psi_{n}\leq\epsilon/2\leq 1
, after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, one has
C\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}\leq\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}/4=1-\rho
, which implies

\big(1+C\sqrt{r\kappa}\phi_{n}\big)(2-\rho)\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\big(1+2-2\rho)(3-2\rho)\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq(2\rho-1)\rho^{t}\psi_{n}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

This also allows us to bound the second term by
(1-\rho)\rho^{t}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}
. Taking together we know (
C.13
) holds, which thus proves
11
for the
(t+1)
th iterate and closes the induction.

C.3
Proof of Theorem
3

We prove in this subsection the stronger contraction statement under the localization
\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. Accordingly, throughout the proof we invoke Assumptions
1
,
2
,
B.5
, and
B.6
with
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. The
\ell_{2}
-only part of Theorem
3
, where only Assumptions
1
–
2
are imposed with
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(2)}(\epsilon)
, is proved later in Section
C.4
by the same argument after removing the row-wise estimates.
Throughout the proof, we use the population first-order condition

\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})=\mathbb{E}\,\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}.

In what follows, besides proving Theorem
3
, we also establish error bounds for the local empirical minimizer that serves as the contraction target of the noisy gradient descent iterates. Specifically, define
\widehat{\bm{Z}}=\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})
, and let
\widehat{\bm{R}}=\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r}}\|\widehat{\bm{Z}}\bm{R}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
. Under the assumptions and scaling conditions used to prove the
\ell_{\infty}
error contraction in Theorem
3
, we show that the following holds for
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
with probability at least
1-\delta
:

{\|\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\quad\text{and}\quad{\|\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

(C.14)

Moreover, Step 2 proves the first-order condition
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})={\bm{0}}
. The results are then used in Step 3 in three ways: first, to define the effective initialization radii
\phi_{n}^{\dagger},\psi_{n}^{\dagger}
; second, to transfer the conditioning and row-scale bounds from
\bm{Z}^{*}
to
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
; and third, to convert contraction toward
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
into the final error bounds relative to
\bm{Z}^{*}
.

Fix the radius
\epsilon
in the theorem, and then we can choose an auxiliary radius
\varepsilon>0
such that

\frac{1}{c_{0}}\Big(\sqrt{r\kappa}\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\vee\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\Big)\leq\varepsilon\leq\frac{c_{0}}{(\beta/\alpha+\sqrt{r})\sqrt{r}\,\kappa}\,\epsilon,

(C.15)

where
c_{0}>0
is sufficiently small.
Such a choice of
\varepsilon
is possible because the first two noise
scaling conditions in Theorem
3
imply that
the lower bound in (
C.15
) is smaller than the
upper bound, after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary. With
\zeta_{r}:=\big(\tfrac{\beta}{\alpha}+\sqrt{r}\big)\sqrt{r}\kappa
, we may also write

\frac{\sqrt{r}\,\kappa}{\alpha}\varepsilon\leq c_{0},\quad\zeta_{r}\varepsilon\leq c_{0}\epsilon,\quad\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq c_{0}\,\frac{\epsilon}{\zeta_{r}},\quad\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq c_{0}\frac{\epsilon\zeta_{r}}{\sqrt{r\kappa}},

(C.16)

for sufficiently small
c_{0}
.

We next introduce a smaller compact neighborhood
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)
to construct a stationary point by minimizing the gradient norm on a compact set, and a slightly larger neighborhood
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
on which the local curvature and Lipschitz bounds will be invoked. In particular, define

\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)=\Big\{\bm{Z}:\frac{\|\bm{Z}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\varepsilon,\ \ \frac{\|\bm{Z}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}\leq\epsilon\Big\},

(C.17)

and

\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)=\Big\{\bm{Z}:\|\bm{Z}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\epsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},\ \ \|\bm{Z}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\epsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\Big\}.

(C.18)

Since
\varepsilon\leq\epsilon
, we have

\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)\subseteq\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)\subseteq\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon).

For
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})
, by definition of
\widehat{\bm{R}}
, we know
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. Our first goal is to construct a local reference point that can later be shown to satisfy the first-order condition and then equals
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
with high probability. In particular, we define

\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{Z}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)}\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})\|_{2\to\infty}^{2}.

Such a minimizer exists because
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)
is compact and

\bm{Z}\mapsto\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})\|_{2\to\infty}^{2}
is continuous.
Let

\bm{E}:=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}.

The rest of the proof consists of three steps. The first two steps study the local reference point
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
. Step 3 then proves that the gradient descent iterates contract toward this point. Once Step 2 establishes
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}=\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
, the theorem follows by combining the contraction toward
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
with the statistical error bounds for
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
. Specifically, the proof is organized as follows.

(1)

construct an interior stationary point
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
and establish the first-order optimality condition;

(2)

establish the equivalence
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}=\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
and the bounds for
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
, which proves (
C.14
);

(3)

prove contraction of
\bm{Z}^{t}
toward
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
, or equivalently, towards
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
.

Step 1: Construct an interior stationary point
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
and establish first-order optimality.

Since
\bm{Z}^{*}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)
and
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
, the minimizing property of
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
implies

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|_{2\to\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|_{2\to\infty}=2\|\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}

(C.19)

\displaystyle=2\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq 2\sqrt{n}\,\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|,

where the last inequality follows from the first part of Equation (
17b
).
Consequently,

\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\sqrt{n}\,\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|_{2\to\infty}\leq 2n\,\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|.

(C.20)

For
s\in[0,1]
, let

\bm{Z}_{s}:=\bm{Z}^{*}+s\bm{E}.

Since
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)\subseteq\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
, the whole segment
\{\bm{Z}_{s}:0\leq s\leq 1\}
lies in
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)\subseteq\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
.
Applying the integral mean value theorem to

\mathrm{vec}\big(\{\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)\}^{\intercal}\big)
along the segment from
\bm{Z}^{*}
to
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}\;\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}^{\intercal})}_{\gamma_{1}}+\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\;\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}^{\intercal})}_{\gamma_{2}}+\underbrace{\bar{\mathcal{G}}\;\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}^{\intercal})}_{\gamma_{3}}=\underbrace{n^{-1}\mathrm{vec}\Big(\big\{\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})-\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\big\}^{\intercal}\Big)}_{\gamma_{4}},

(C.21)

where
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}
,
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}
and
\bar{\mathcal{G}}
are defined as

\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}=n^{-1}\int_{0}^{1}\Big\{\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{s})-2\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{s})\otimes\bm{I}_{r}\Big\}\,ds,

\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}=\frac{2}{n}\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{s})\otimes\bm{I}_{r}\,ds,\qquad\bar{\mathcal{G}}=\frac{2}{n}\int_{0}^{1}\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{s})\otimes\bm{I}_{r}\,ds.

We now bound
\gamma_{1}
–
\gamma_{4}
.

For
\gamma_{1}
, Lemma
2
gives

\lambda_{\min}\!\Big(n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{s})-2n^{-1}\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{s})\otimes\bm{I}_{r}\Big)\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-4n^{-1}\|\bm{Z}_{s}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

for every
s\in[0,1]
. Since
\|\bm{Z}_{s}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=s\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\varepsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
and
\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq nr\kappa\sigma_{\min}
,

\lambda_{\min}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}})\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-4\varepsilon^{2}r\kappa\sigma_{\min}\geq\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{2},

after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, where we used
\varepsilon\sqrt{r}\,\kappa/\alpha\leq c_{0}
.

For
\gamma_{2}
, Equation
17a
yields

\|\gamma_{2}\|\leq\frac{2}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{s})\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 2\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

For
\gamma_{3}
, using
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
, Assumption
2
with
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})
in place of
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})
, and Lemma
5
, one can check

\displaystyle\|\gamma_{3}\|\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{2}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|(\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{s})-\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*}))\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{2L_{2}}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bm{Z}_{s}\bm{Z}_{s}^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\,\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{CL_{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{n}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

where the last step uses
\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\varepsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq c_{0}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|
.

For
\gamma_{4}
, by (
C.20
),

\displaystyle\|\gamma_{4}\|

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|_{\mathrm{F}}+n^{-1}\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|+2n^{-1}\|\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle=2\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|+2n^{-1}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+2\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Here, the last inequality follows from
n^{-1}\|\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq n^{-1}\|\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
by (
17a
).

Combining the bounds for
\gamma_{1}
–
\gamma_{4}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}})^{-1}\big(\|\gamma_{2}\|+\|\gamma_{3}\|+\|\gamma_{4}\|\big)

(C.22)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)+\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+C\frac{L_{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}n}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

By the lower bound in (
C.15
), or equivalently
by the first two bounds in (
C.16
),
we have

\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq c_{0}\varepsilon,\qquad\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq\frac{c_{0}}{\sqrt{r\kappa}}\varepsilon\leq c_{0}\varepsilon,

and the localization of
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
such that

\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}n}\cdot\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\kappa n\sigma_{\min}}\sqrt{r\kappa n\sigma_{\min}}}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}n}\,\varepsilon=\varepsilon\frac{\kappa\sqrt{r}}{\alpha}\leq c_{0},

we can shrink
c_{0}
if necessary and absorb the last two terms on the right-hand side of (
C.22
). This in turn gives

\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}=\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}.

(C.23)

Then it remains to establish the
\ell_{\infty}
bound. For
i,j\in[n]
, define

\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ij}=n^{-1}\int_{0}^{1}\Big\{\nabla_{\bm{z}_{i}\bm{z}_{j}}^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}_{s}\bm{Z}_{s}^{\intercal})-2G_{ij}(\bm{Z}_{s})\bm{I}_{r}\Big\}\,ds,

\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{ij}=\frac{2}{n}\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{G}_{ij}(\bm{Z}_{s})\bm{I}_{r}\,ds,\qquad\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{ij}=\frac{2}{n}\int_{0}^{1}\bar{G}_{ij}(\bm{Z}_{s})\bm{I}_{r}\,ds,

and

\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}=\int_{0}^{1}\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{s})\,ds,\qquad\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{i,\cdot}:=(\widetilde{\mathcal{P}})_{\mathcal{R}_{i},\cdot},\qquad\mathcal{R}_{i}=\{(i-1)r+1,\dots,ir\}.

Then (
C.21
) is equivalent to the row-wise decomposition

\displaystyle\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii}(\bm{E}_{i,\cdot})^{\intercal}}_{\delta_{1,i}}+\underbrace{\sum_{j\neq i}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ij}(\bm{E}_{j,\cdot})^{\intercal}}_{\delta_{2,i}}+\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bar{\mathcal{G}}_{ij}(\bm{E}_{j,\cdot})^{\intercal}}_{\delta_{3,i}}+\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{n}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}_{ij}(\bm{E}_{j,\cdot})^{\intercal}}_{\delta_{4,i}}+\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{i,\cdot}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}^{\intercal})}_{\delta_{5,i}}

(C.24)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{n^{-1}\big\{\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})-\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\big\}_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}}_{\delta_{6,i}}.

We arrange the decomposition as such to emphasize that
\delta_{1,i}
–
\delta_{3,i}
can be treated similarly to
\delta_{1,t}
–
\delta_{3,t}
in the proof of Theorem
1
, and
\delta_{4,i}
–
\delta_{6,i}
arise from statistical noise.

Let

\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}:={\rm diag}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{11},\dots,\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{nn}),\qquad\|\bm{A}\|_{\infty,r}:=\max_{i\in[n]}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\bm{A}_{\mathcal{R}_{i},\mathcal{R}_{j}}\|,\text{ for }\bm{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{nr\times nr}

By Lemma
3
, (
C.23
), and
\varepsilon\leq\epsilon
, we have that

(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}\leq\min_{i\in[n]}\lambda_{\min}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii})\leq\max_{i\in[n]}\lambda_{\max}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii})\leq C(\alpha+\beta)\kappa\sigma_{\min},

after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary. Hence, one has
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\|_{\infty,r}\leq\{(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}\}^{-1}
.
Therefore, (
C.24
) yields

\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\frac{1}{(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\max_{i\in[n]}\sum_{\ell=2}^{6}\|\delta_{\ell,i}\|.

(C.25)

We now bound
\delta_{2,i}
–
\delta_{6,i}
.

For
\delta_{2,i}
, the same argument as in the
\delta_{2,t}
bound in the proof of Theorem
1
gives

\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{2,i}\|\leq C\beta n^{-1}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bm{Z}_{s}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{Z}_{s}\|\,\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq C\beta\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\,\varepsilon\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty},

where we used

\|\bm{Z}_{s}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq(1+c_{0})\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}
,
\|\bm{Z}_{s}\|\leq(1+c_{0})\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|
,
\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\varepsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
, and
\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|=\sqrt{n\kappa\sigma_{\min}}
.

For
\delta_{3,i}
, using
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
, Assumption
B.6
with
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})
in place of
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})
, one can obtain

\|\bm{Z}_{s}\bm{Z}_{s}^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+(\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|+\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}})\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{3,i}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{s})-\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|_{2\to\infty}\,\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2L_{\infty}}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bm{Z}_{s}\bm{Z}_{s}^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{2\to\infty}\,\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq CL_{\infty}\varepsilon^{2}r\kappa\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+CL_{\infty}\varepsilon\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}.

For
\delta_{4,i}
, (
17b
) and the theorem’s scaling conditions give
n^{-1}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})\|_{\infty\to 1}\leq\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\leq\alpha\sigma_{\min}/4
uniformly over
\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. Since
\bm{Z}_{s}\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
for all
s\in[0,1]
,

\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{4,i}\|\leq\frac{2}{n}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{s})\|_{\infty\to 1}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq 2\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{2}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Remark 2
.

The term
\delta_{4,i}
can be merged with
\delta_{1,i}
, which allows for a larger level of statistical noise. In particular, we assume
\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\leq C\alpha\sigma_{\min}
for some constant
C
, rather than imposing the more restrictive condition
\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\leq\alpha\sigma_{\min}/4
. When
\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\gg\alpha\sigma_{\min}
, handling the noise typically requires exploiting additional problem structure and more delicate analysis; see, for example, Section
A.2
. Here, we present a simple argument that allows
\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\asymp\alpha\sigma_{\min}

Note that

\delta_{1,i}+\delta_{4,i}=\{\underbrace{\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}}_{:=\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{Q}}\}_{\mathcal{R}_{i},}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}^{\intercal}).

Here,
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}={\rm diag}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{11},\dots,\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{nn})
. Similarly, one can invert
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{Q}
in (
C.24
) to yield

\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{Q}^{-1}\big\|_{\infty,r}\times\max_{i\in[n]}\big(\big\|\delta_{2,i}\big\|+\big\|\delta_{3,i}\big\|+\big\|\delta_{5,i}\big\|+\big\|\delta_{6,i}\big\|\big).

(C.26)

With Lemma
3
,
\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\epsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\epsilon\sqrt{nr\kappa\sigma_{\min}}
, scaling condition
\epsilon^{2}r\kappa\leq c_{0}\alpha
, and the continuity of eigenvalues, one can check that

(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}\leq\min_{i\in[n]}\lambda_{\min}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii}\big)\leq\max_{i\in[n]}\lambda_{\max}\big(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii}\big)\leq\frac{\beta\kappa\sigma_{\min}}{2}.

(C.27)

The block diagonal structure of
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii}
implies that (
C.27
) also holds when replacing
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii}
with
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}
. Note that with (
17a
), similar to bounding
\gamma_{2}
above, we know
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\|\leq 2\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)
. With
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})\leq c_{0}
for sufficiently small
c_{0}
, we know

\frac{\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\|}{\lambda_{\min}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii})}\leq\frac{2\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}<1.

Thus, the inverse of
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}
can be expressed via the Neumann series

\displaystyle(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}})^{-1}=\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}+\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}+\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}+\cdots,

and

\big\|(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}})^{-1}\big\|\leq\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\big\|\big(\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\|\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\|\big)^{l}\leq\frac{C}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}.

Next, by the second part of (
17b
), we know

\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\|_{\infty,r}\leq\frac{1}{n}\max_{\bm{Z}=\bm{Z}^{*}+s\bm{E},s\in[0,1]}2\|\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})-\mathbb{E}\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})\|_{\infty\to 1}\leq 2\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\leq C\alpha\sigma_{\min}

With
(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1})_{\mathcal{R}_{i},}=({\bm{0}}_{r\times(i-1)r},\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii}^{-1},{\bm{0}}_{r\times(n-i)r})
,
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\|_{\infty,r}\leq\lambda_{\min}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii})^{-1}\leq\{(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}\}^{-1}
Consequently,
\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1})^{l}
can be bounded as follows

\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1})^{l}\big\|_{\infty,r}\leq\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\big\|_{\infty,r}\big\|\Big(\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\big\|_{\infty,r}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\big\|_{\infty,r}\Big)^{l}\leq\frac{1}{(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\Big(\frac{C}{1-c_{0}}\Big)^{l}.

(C.28)

Moreover, since
\|\cdot\|_{\infty,r}\leq\sqrt{n}\max_{i\in[n]}\|(\cdot)_{\mathcal{R}_{i},}\|
, we can bound it by

\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1})^{l}\big\|_{\infty,r}\leq\sqrt{n}\max_{i\in[n]}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{ii}^{-1}\big\|(\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\big\|\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}\big\|\big)^{l}\leq\frac{\sqrt{n}}{(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\Big(\frac{2\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\Big)^{l}.

(C.29)

Let

l_{0}=\left\lceil\frac{\log n}{2\log\big\{4C\alpha\sigma_{\min}/\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)\big\}}\right\rceil=o(\log n)

as
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})\leq c_{0}
for any sufficiently small
c_{0}
. Use (
C.28
) when
l\leq l_{0}
and (
C.29
) when
l>l_{0}
to bound
\|(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}})^{-1}\|_{\infty,r}
by

\|(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}})^{-1}\|_{\infty,r}\leq\sum_{l=0}^{\infty}\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1}(\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}^{-1})^{l}\big\|_{\infty,r}\leq\frac{C^{l_{0}}}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq\frac{n^{o(1)}}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}.

(C.30)

This bound depends on
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})
. When
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})\asymp n^{-c}
for any constant
c
, we know

\|(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}}_{LD}+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}})^{-1}\|_{\infty,r}\leq\frac{C}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}.

For simplicity, we present Theorem
3
under small noise condition
\bar{\Delta}_{\infty}(n,\delta)\leq\alpha\sigma_{\min}/4
.

For
\delta_{5,i}
, we first compute the second-order derivative of
\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}p^{*}_{\alpha}(\bm{Z})
as

\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})=\frac{\alpha n}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{l=1}^{r}(\bm{\vartheta}_{lk}-\bm{\vartheta}_{kl})(\bm{\vartheta}_{lk}-\bm{\vartheta}_{kl})^{\intercal},

where for each
lk,\in[r]
,
\bm{\vartheta}_{lk}=n^{-1/2}\big(z_{1l}^{*}\bm{e}_{k}^{\intercal},\dots,z_{nl}^{*}\bm{e}_{k}^{\intercal}\big)^{\intercal}
. Hence
\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}=\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})
is deterministic and subsequently

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathcal{P}}_{i,\cdot}\mathrm{vec}(\bm{E}^{\intercal})\big\|

\displaystyle=\Big\|\frac{\alpha}{2n}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\sum_{l=1}^{r}\big(z_{il}^{*}\bm{e}_{k}^{\intercal}-z_{ik}^{*}\bm{e}_{l}^{\intercal}\big)\big((\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\big)_{l,k}\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha}{2n}\Big\{\sum_{k,l\in[r]}\|z_{il}^{*}\bm{e}_{k}^{\intercal}-z_{ik}^{*}\bm{e}_{l}^{\intercal}\|^{2}\Big\}^{1/2}\big\|(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha r\kappa\sigma_{\min}\,\varepsilon\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty},

uniformly over
i\in[n]
, where we used
\big\|(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}^{*}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 2\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\,\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
.

For
\delta_{6,i}
, by (
C.19
),

\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{6,i}\|\leq n^{-1}\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|_{2\to\infty}+n^{-1}\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|_{2\to\infty}\leq 4\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Substituting these bounds into (
C.25
) and multiplying by
(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}
yields

\displaystyle(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq

\displaystyle\,\max_{i\in[n]}\big(\|\delta_{2,i}\|+\|\delta_{3,i}\|+\|\delta_{4,i}\|+\|\delta_{5,i}\|+\|\delta_{6,i}\|\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,C\alpha\sigma_{\min}\left\{\Big(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\sqrt{r}\Big)\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\,\varepsilon+\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\right\}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{2}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}+CL_{\infty}\varepsilon\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Here the
\varepsilon^{2}r\kappa
contribution from
\delta_{3,i}
is absorbed into the displayed
\zeta_{r}\varepsilon
term by
\varepsilon\sqrt{r}\,\kappa/\alpha\leq c_{0}
. By (
C.16
), we have the following

\frac{CL_{\infty}\varepsilon\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}}{(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq\frac{CL_{\infty}c_{0}}{1-c_{0}}\leq\frac{1}{8},\quad\text{ and }\quad\frac{1}{2(1-c_{0})}\leq\frac{5}{8}.

Therefore we obtain

\Big(1-\frac{3}{4}\Big)\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}\leq C\Big(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\sqrt{r}\Big)\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\,\varepsilon+C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}.

By (
C.16
), the right-hand side is at most
\epsilon/8
after choosing
c_{0}
small.

\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}=\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2},

(C.31)

after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, where the last inequality uses
\zeta_{r}\varepsilon\leq c_{0}\epsilon
and
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})\leq c_{0}\epsilon
, which follows from
(
C.16
).

Combining (
C.23
) and (
C.31
), we see that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
is a strict interior point of
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)
. Therefore,
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
is a strict interior local minimizer of

\bm{Z}\mapsto\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})\|_{2\to\infty}^{2}\qquad\text{over }\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon).

We next show that
h_{\alpha}^{*}
is strongly convex on
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. By Lemma
2
,

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{Z}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)}\lambda_{\min}\!\Big(n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})-2n^{-1}\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})\otimes\bm{I}_{r}\Big)

\displaystyle\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-4n^{-1}\epsilon^{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}-4\epsilon^{2}r\kappa\sigma_{\min}

\displaystyle\geq(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}.

Here, the last inequality follows from
\epsilon\leq c_{0}\alpha/(\kappa\sqrt{r})
and shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary. Moreover, for
\bm{Z}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)\subseteq\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
, (
17a
), Assumption
2
with
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})
in place of
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})
, and Lemma
5
give

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})\|+\frac{1}{n}\|\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})-\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|

\displaystyle\leq\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)+\frac{L_{2}}{n}\|\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)+CL_{2}\epsilon\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\leq c_{0}\alpha\sigma_{\min},

after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, where the last steps uses (
C.16
). Hence, by Weyl’s inequality, one can obtain

\min_{\bm{Z}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)}\lambda_{\min}\!\big(n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})\big)\geq\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{4}>0.

At this point, we have shown that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
lies strictly inside the auxiliary neighborhood. It remains to upgrade this minimizing property to the first-order condition. In particular, we introduce the following lemma.

Lemma 7
.

Let
\mathcal{D}\subseteq\mathbb{R}^{n\times r}
be compact, and let
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\in\mathcal{D}
be a strict interior local minimizer of

\bm{Z}\mapsto\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})\|_{2\to\infty}^{2}

over
\mathcal{D}
. If
\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})
is positive definite, then

\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})={\bm{0}}.

Proof.

See Section
F.2
. ∎

Since
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)
is a strict interior local minimizer of
\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z})\|_{2\to\infty}^{2}
and
n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\succeq\alpha\sigma_{\min}\bm{I}/4
within
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
, Lemma
7
yields

\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})={\bm{0}}.

(C.32)

In addition,
h_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)
is strongly convex on
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
, so
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
is the unique minimizer of
h_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)
on
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
.

Step 2: Sharpen the statistical rates and identify
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}=\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
.

We now sharpen the bounds for
\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
and
\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}
, where recall
\bm{E}=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}
. We first sharpen the Frobenius error. Returning to (
C.21
), Step 1 gives
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})={\bm{0}}
, so

\gamma_{4}=-\mathrm{vec}\!\Bigl(\bigl\{n^{-1}\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bigr\}^{{\intercal}}\Bigr).

Since
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
and
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})={\bm{0}}
, one then has by (
17a
) that

n^{-1}\|\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|_{\mathrm{F}}=2n^{-1}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 2\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Hence, using the bounds for
\gamma_{2}
and
\gamma_{3}
from Step 1 together with
\lambda_{\min}(\widetilde{\mathcal{H}})\geq\alpha\sigma_{\min}/2
, it holds that

\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+C\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}n}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

(C.33)

Dividing both sides by
\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
, and using the Step 1 bound
{\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}/{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\leq{\varepsilon}/{2}
,
we get

\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}n}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}n}\cdot\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\leq C\frac{\sqrt{r}\,\kappa}{\alpha}\,\varepsilon\leq Cc_{0}.

Since
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})\leq c_{0}
, after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, the last two terms in (
C.33
) can be absorbed into the left-hand side. Therefore,

\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}.

(C.34)

This sharper Frobenius bound also yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\mathcal{G}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|+\frac{1}{n}\|\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})-\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})\|

(C.35)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)+CL_{2}\,n^{-1}\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}-\bm{Z}^{*}(\bm{Z}^{*})^{\intercal}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{8},

after shrinking
c_{0}
further, where we used Lemma
5
and (
C.34
), and the last inequality again uses
\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})\leq c_{0}
.

We next sharpen the row-wise
\ell_{\infty}
bound. Returning to (
C.25
), the only difference from Step 1 is that we may now use (
C.34
) in place of the crude bound
\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\varepsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
. The same calculations as in Step 1 give

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{2,i}\|

\displaystyle\leq C\beta n^{-1}\|\bm{E}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\max_{s\in[0,1]}\|\bm{Z}^{*}+s\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\bm{Z}^{*}+s\bm{E}\|

\displaystyle\leq C\beta\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\,\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty},

and similarly

\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{5,i}\|\leq C\alpha\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\,\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

For the deterministic term
\delta_{3,i}
, the bound in Step 1 combined with (
C.34
) implies

\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{3,i}\|\leq\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{8}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}+C\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\sigma_{\min}\,\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\,\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty},

after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary.
The noise term
\delta_{4,i}
and score term
\delta_{6,i}
remain unchanged:

\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{4,i}\|\leq\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{2}\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty},\qquad\max_{i\in[n]}\|\delta_{6,i}\|\leq 4\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Substituting these bounds into (
C.25
), and shrinking
c_{0}
so that

\frac{1}{(1-c_{0})\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\Big(\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{8}+\frac{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}{2}\Big)\leq\frac{3}{4},

we arrive at

\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}\leq\frac{3}{4}\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}+C\Big(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}+\sqrt{r}\Big)\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\,\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}+C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}.

Using the theorem scaling condition

\Big(\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\sqrt{\kappa}+\sqrt{r}\Big)\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\,\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}\leq c_{0},

we thus conclude that

\frac{\|\bm{E}\|_{2\to\infty}}{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}.

(C.36)

We now show that the penalty must vanish at
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
:

p_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})=0,

(C.37)

so that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
is also stationary for the original loss.
Suppose not. Let
\widetilde{\bm{R}}\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r}}\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\bm{R}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
. By (
C.34
),

\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\leq C\frac{\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq C\sqrt{r\,\kappa}\,\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq Cc_{0}<0.01

for sufficiently small
c_{0}
. Hence Lemma
6
applies and yields

\|\widetilde{\bm{R}}-\bm{I}_{r}\|\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{R}}-\bm{I}_{r}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq C\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}(\bm{Z}^{*})}\leq C\sqrt{r\,\kappa}\,\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\leq Cc_{0}\,\varepsilon,

where the last step uses the lower bound for
\varepsilon
in (
C.15
). Now combine this with the strict interior bounds from Step 1:

\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},\qquad\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Then, one can check that

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{R}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\|\,\|\widetilde{\bm{R}}-\bm{I}_{r}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+(\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|+\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}})\,Cc_{0}\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq\Big(\frac{1}{2}+Cc_{0}(1+\varepsilon)\Big)\varepsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}<\varepsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

for
c_{0}
small enough. Likewise, since
\varepsilon\leq\epsilon
,

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{R}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\|_{2\to\infty}\|\widetilde{\bm{R}}-\bm{I}_{r}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+(\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty})\,Cc_{0}\varepsilon

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+Cc_{0}\varepsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}<\epsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Hence
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{R}}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\varepsilon,\epsilon)\subseteq\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
.
By Lemma
4
, we have
p_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{R}})=0
. Since
(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{R}})(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{R}})^{\intercal}=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}
, it follows that

h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{R}})=\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})<\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})+p_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})=h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}),

contradicting the fact that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
is the unique minimizer of
h_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)
within
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
as establish at the end of Step 1. This proves (
C.37
).
Next, since Step 1 established
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})={\bm{0}}
, and the gradient of
p_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)
vanishes whenever
p_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)=0
, we conclude that

\nabla_{\bm{Z}}\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})=2\mathcal{G}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\widetilde{\bm{Z}}={\bm{0}}.

(C.38)

We finally establish the equivalence between
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
and
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
. Recall that
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{Z}\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})
, and let
\widehat{\bm{R}}\in\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r}}\|\widehat{\bm{Z}}\bm{R}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
. By definition,
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
, and Lemma
4
gives
p_{\alpha}^{*}(\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}})=0.

Since
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\in\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)\subseteq\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
and
\widehat{\bm{Z}}
minimizes
\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})
over
\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
, we have
\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})\leq\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})
. On the other hand, Step 1 showed that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
is the unique minimizer of
h_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)
on
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
. It therefore follows that

h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}})=\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}).

Invoke (
C.37
) and we have
h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})=\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})
, which then implies
\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})\leq\mathcal{L}(\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})\leq\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})
.
Thus equality holds throughout, so

h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})=h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}).

By uniqueness of the minimizer of
h_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)
on
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
, we conclude that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}=\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
. Consequently,

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\|\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

(C.39)

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}=\|\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

This proves the (
C.14
), and also shows that the aligned solution
\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
is unique.

Step 3:
\ell_{2}
- and
\ell_{\infty}
-error contractions for
\bm{Z}^{t}
.

It remains to prove that the iterates contract toward the local stationary point
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
constructed in Steps 1–2. Unlike the noiseless case, in the noisy setting the iterates are not expected to contract exactly to
\bm{Z}^{*}
; instead, Step 2 identified
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}=\widehat{\bm{Z}}\widehat{\bm{R}}
and showed that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
is already within statistical error of
\bm{Z}^{*}
. Therefore, once we prove geometric contraction toward
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
, the theorem follows by combining this contraction with (
C.39
) and the triangle inequality. Moreover, implied by the strong convexity of
h_{\alpha}^{*}(\cdot)
within
\bar{\mathcal{D}}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
and uniqueness of
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
as the minimizer, establishing convergence to
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
shall be a reasonable choice.

To absorb the statistical shift from
\bm{Z}^{*}
to
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
, we slightly enlarge the initialization radii by the estimator error from Step 2 and define the effective radii

\phi_{n}^{\dagger}:=\phi_{n}+C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}},\qquad\psi_{n}^{\dagger}:=\psi_{n}+C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}},

where
C>0
is a sufficiently large universal constant.
We use the notation
\phi_{n}^{\dagger}
and
\psi_{n}^{\dagger}
to emphasize that the convergence to
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
admits an additional noise term. By the initialization bounds (
8
) and (
10
), the scaling conditions in Theorem
3
, and the estimator bounds (
C.39
), after enlarging
C
and shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary, the effective radii satisfy the same scaling conditions:

\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\leq\frac{2}{3}\epsilon\wedge c_{0}\frac{\alpha}{\kappa\sqrt{r}},\qquad\psi_{n}^{\dagger}\leq\frac{2}{3}\epsilon,\qquad\frac{\beta}{\alpha}\kappa^{3/2}\,\sqrt{r}\,\frac{\phi_{n}^{\dagger}}{\psi_{n}^{\dagger}}\leq c_{0}.

(C.40)

For the last inequality, we use

\frac{\phi_{n}^{\dagger}}{\psi_{n}^{\dagger}}\leq C\left\{\frac{\phi_{n}}{\psi_{n}}+\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}\right\},

where the first term on the right side is bounded by the condition on the initialization condition in the theorem, and the second term can be bounded by the theorem’s scaling condition.
Based on the above discussion, we know that to establish the theorem, it only suffices to show that the iterates contract geometrically toward
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
with these effective radii. In particular, we show in the following that for all
t\geq 0
,

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq\rho^{t}\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},\qquad dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}^{t},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq\rho^{t}\psi_{n}^{\dagger}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty},

(C.41)

where
\rho=1-\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}/4
.
The base case
t=0
follows from the triangle inequality and (
C.39
):

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{0},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{0},\bm{Z}^{*})+\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

and similarly,

dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}^{0},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}^{0},\bm{Z}^{*})+\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\psi_{n}^{\dagger}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

Now assume (
C.41
) holds at iteration
t
. To establish the error contraction in (
C.41
), we align the current iterate with the new target
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
and work under the corresponding alignment error. In particular, for each
t\geq 0
, define

\bm{R}_{t}^{\dagger}:=\mathop{\mathrm{argmin}}_{\bm{R}\in\mathcal{O}^{r}}\|\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}-\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\|_{\mathrm{F}},\qquad\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}:=\bm{Z}^{t}\bm{R}_{t}^{\dagger},\qquad\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}:=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}-\widetilde{\bm{Z}}.

By Lemma
4
, we have
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t{\intercal}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
.
To reuse the contraction argument from Step 1 with
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
in place of
\bm{Z}^{*}
, we recenter the alignment penalty at
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
by defining

p_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\bm{Z}):=\frac{\alpha n^{2}}{4}\big\|n^{-1}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}\bm{Z}-n^{-1}\bm{Z}^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\big\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},\qquad h_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\bm{Z}):=\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})+p_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\bm{Z}).

Then
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t})={\bm{0}}\qquad\text{for all }t\geq 0
. Moreover, Step 1 gives
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})={\bm{0}}
, and Step 2 gives
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}\mathcal{L}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal})={\bm{0}}
. Since also
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})=0
, we have

\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})={\bm{0}}.

We next record that
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
inherits the same basic conditioning and row scale as
\bm{Z}^{*}
.
By (
C.39
), Weyl’s inequality, and
\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq c_{0}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}
, we also have

\sigma_{r}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\geq(1-c_{0})\sqrt{n\sigma_{\min}},\qquad\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\|\leq(1+c_{0})\sqrt{n\kappa\sigma_{\min}},\qquad\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq(1+c_{0})\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

(C.42)

For
s\in[0,1]
, similarly define

\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s):=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}+s\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}.

Provided that the induction hypothesis (
C.41
) holds for
t
, with (
C.39
), we know

\displaystyle dist_{2}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+s\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\Big(C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}+\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\Big)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

\displaystyle dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s),\bm{Z}^{*})\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}+s\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\Big(C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}+\psi_{n}^{\dagger}\Big)\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}.

By (
C.40
) and the scaling conditions for
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)
and
\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)
, we know

C\frac{\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}+\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\leq\epsilon,\qquad C\frac{\Delta_{\infty}(n,\delta)}{\alpha\sigma_{\min}}+\psi_{n}^{\dagger}\leq\epsilon.

Then both right-hand sides are bounded by
\epsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
and
\epsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{2\to\infty}
, respectively, after shrinking
c_{0}
. Hence
\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s)\in\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
for all
s\in[0,1]
. Therefore, all assumptions stated with
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{D}_{z}^{(\infty)}(\epsilon)
are available along this interpolation path. Now we let

\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t+1}^{(t)}:=\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{\dagger}-\widetilde{\bm{Z}}.

Using the gradient update,
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})=0
, and
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t})=0
, we therefore obtain

\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t+1}^{(t)}=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}-\widetilde{\bm{Z}}-\frac{\eta}{n}\Big\{\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t})-\nabla_{\bm{Z}}h_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\Big\}.

In what follows, we repeat the proof of
\ell_{2}
and
\ell_{\infty}
error contractions similar to the proof in Section
C.2
.

\ell_{2}
error contraction.

Applying the fundamental theorem of calculus (Theorem 4.2 in
28
, Chapter XIII) along the segment
\{\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s):0\leq s\leq 1\}
, and splitting off the term
2\mathcal{G}(\cdot)\otimes\bm{I}_{r}
, we obtain

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq\widetilde{\gamma}_{1,t}^{\dagger}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2,t}^{\dagger}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{3,t}^{\dagger},

where

\widetilde{\gamma}_{1,t}^{\dagger}:=\left\|\bigl(\bm{I}_{nr}-\eta\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}^{\dagger}\bigr)\mathrm{vec}(\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}^{\intercal})\right\|,\qquad\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}^{\dagger}:=n^{-1}\int_{0}^{1}\Big\{\nabla_{\bm{z}}^{2}h_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))-2\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))\otimes\bm{I}_{r}\Big\}\,ds,

\widetilde{\gamma}_{2,t}^{\dagger}:=\frac{2\eta}{n}\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\bigl\{\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))-\mathcal{G}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\bigr\}\,ds\;\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}},\qquad\widetilde{\gamma}_{3,t}^{\dagger}:=\frac{2\eta}{n}\|\mathcal{G}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

The proof of Step 1 in Theorem
1
applies verbatim with
\bm{Z}^{*}
replaced by
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
. Using (
C.42
), we obtain
\lambda_{\min}(\bar{\mathcal{A}}_{t}^{\dagger})\geq\frac{7}{8}\alpha\sigma_{\min}
, after shrinking
c_{0}
if necessary. Therefore, one has

\widetilde{\gamma}_{1,t}^{\dagger}\leq\Bigl(1-\frac{7}{8}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\Bigr)\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

For
\widetilde{\gamma}_{2,t}^{\dagger}
, we note that
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})=\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})+\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})
. Then, with Assumption
2
with
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})
in place of
\mathcal{G}(\bm{Z})
, Lemma
5
, the localization
\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\epsilon\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}
, and (
17a
), it holds that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\gamma}_{2,t}^{\dagger}\leq

\displaystyle\,C\eta\frac{\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\|}{n}\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+C\eta\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,C\eta\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\sqrt{r}\,\kappa\,\sigma_{\min}\,\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+C\eta\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\,\frac{1}{8}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

where the last inequality uses
\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\leq c_{0}\alpha/(\kappa\sqrt{r})
from (
C.40
) and
\Delta_{2}(n,\delta)/(\alpha\sigma_{\min})\leq c_{0}
, which follows from the theorem’s noise scaling.

For
\widetilde{\gamma}_{3,t}^{\dagger}
, (
C.35
) implies

\widetilde{\gamma}_{3,t}^{\dagger}\leq 2\eta\,\frac{1}{n}\|\mathcal{G}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\|\,\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{4}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

Combining the above bounds, we conclude that

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq\Bigl(1-\frac{1}{2}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\Bigr)\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\rho\,\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\rho^{t+1}\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}},

which therefore implies with the induction hypothesis that

dist_{2}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq\rho^{t+1}\phi_{n}^{\dagger}\|\bm{Z}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.

(C.43)

\ell_{\infty}
error contraction.

As in Step 2 of the proof of Theorem
1
, we have the decomposition

dist_{\infty}(\bm{Z}^{t+1},\widetilde{\bm{Z}})\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}+\|\bm{Z}^{t+1}\bm{R}_{t}^{\dagger}\|_{2\to\infty}\big\|\bm{I}_{r}-(\bm{R}_{t}^{\dagger})^{-1}\bm{R}_{t+1}^{\dagger}\big\|,

(C.44)

where
\bm{R}_{t+1}^{\dagger}
is the optimal alignment of
\bm{Z}^{t+1}
to
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
.

We first bound
\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}
. For
i,j\in[n]
, define

\mathcal{H}_{ij}^{\dagger}(\bm{Z}):=n^{-1}\Bigl\{\nabla_{\bm{z}_{i}\bm{z}_{j}}^{2}\mathcal{L}(\bm{Z}\bm{Z}^{\intercal})-2G_{ij}(\bm{Z})\bm{I}_{r}\Bigr\}.

Since
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t}=\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{t{\intercal}}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
, we have
\widetilde{\bm{Z}}^{\intercal}(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}+s\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t})=(\widetilde{\bm{Z}}+s\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t})^{\intercal}\widetilde{\bm{Z}}
for any
s\in[0,1]
.
Therefore,
p_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))=0
and
\nabla_{\bm{Z}}p_{\alpha,\dagger}^{*}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))=0
for all
s\in[0,1]
. Consequently, the penalty contributes nothing to the rowwise expansion along the interpolation segment. For each
i\in[n]
,

\displaystyle(\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t+1}^{(t)})_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}=\;

\displaystyle\Bigl\{\bm{I}_{r}-\eta\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{H}_{ii}^{\dagger}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))\,ds\Bigr\}(\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t})_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}-\eta\sum_{j\neq i}\int_{0}^{1}\mathcal{H}_{ij}^{\dagger}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))(\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t})_{j,\cdot}^{\intercal}\,ds

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2\eta}{n}\Bigl[\int_{0}^{1}\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))\,ds\;\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\Bigr]_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}-\frac{2\eta}{n}\Bigl[\int_{0}^{1}\bigl\{\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}_{t}^{\dagger}(s))-\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z}^{*})\bigr\}\,ds\;\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\Bigr]_{i,\cdot}^{\intercal}.

As in Step 2 of Theorem
1
, we now bound the four contributions on the right-hand side separately. The first two terms are the diagonal and off-diagonal Hessian contributions, while the last two arise from splitting the gradient term into its stochastic part
\widetilde{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})
and deterministic mean part
\bar{\mathcal{G}}(\bm{Z})
. Subsequently, we denote

\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t+1}^{(t)}\|_{2\to\infty}\leq\widetilde{\delta}_{1,t}^{\dagger}+\widetilde{\delta}_{2,t}^{\dagger}+\widetilde{\delta}_{3,t}^{\dagger}+\widetilde{\delta}_{4,t}^{\dagger}.

We first bound the deterministic Hessian terms
\widetilde{\delta}_{1,t}^{\dagger}
and
\widetilde{\delta}_{2,t}^{\dagger}
. The proofs are identical to those for
\delta_{1,t}
and
\delta_{2,t}
in Step 2 of Theorem
1
.
Specifically, by the same argument and using (
C.42
), we have

\widetilde{\delta}_{1,t}^{\dagger}\leq\Bigl(1-\frac{7}{8}\eta\alpha\sigma_{\min}\Bigr)\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{2\to\infty},

and

\widetilde{\delta}_{2,t}^{\dagger}\leq C\eta\beta\sqrt{\frac{\kappa\sigma_{\min}}{n}}\,\|\widetilde{\bm{Z}}\|_{2\to\infty}\,\|\widetilde{\bm{E}}_{t}\|_{\mathrm{F}}.
